# All-zone map-label catalogue

583 DATs scanned; 582 zone-map DATs; 494 unique rasters; 88 duplicates; 204 mapped zones; 0 decode errors.

294 zones in the available resource registry; 90 have no map in this pack. This does not mean no map exists in the game.

102 rasters have a selected-label visual review; 273 reviewed zone/name pairs in 78 zones. Review is not exhaustive.

**Research only: no world calibration or verified area boundaries.**

Selected geography, streets, squares, rooms and named landmarks; excludes shops, mobs, quest text and zone destinations. Review status is per raster, not an exhaustive zone review.

The candidate text below is UNREVIEWED unless it exactly matches a reviewed name. Hints are heuristic, not classifications: they can still contain mobs, exits, legends and OCR errors. Split lines have not been guessed or joined. An empty review list is not proof of absence.
